(herald "Meta-AMS (MAMS) Registrar Registration")

(comment "CPSA 4.4.7")
(comment "All input read from registrarRegistration.scm")

(defprotocol mams_registrar_reg basic
  (defrole new_registrar
    (vars
      (n1 unit_number reg_role_number reg_ref_number announce_registrar
        text) (app name) (reg_mib locn)
      (n2 conf_role_number conf_ref_number registrar_noted text)
      (cont name) (n3 text) (cell_spec_body mesg))
    (trace
      (send
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number reg_role_number reg_ref_number)
          (cat n1 (enc n1 "known_string" (privk app)))
          announce_registrar))
      (recv
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n2 (enc n2 "known_string" (privk cont)))
          registrar_noted))
      (recv
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n3 (enc n3 "known_string" (privk cont))) cell_spec_body))
      (stor reg_mib cell_spec_body)))
  (defrole config_server
    (vars (n0 text) (app1 name)
      (foreign_role_numbers foreign_ref_numbers n1 unit_number
        reg_role_number reg_ref_number announce_registrar text)
      (app name) (conf_mib locn)
      (n2 conf_role_number conf_ref_number registrar_noted text)
      (cont name) (n3 text) (cell_spec_body mesg) (n4 text))
    (trace
      (recv
        (cat
          (mpdu_header unit_number foreign_role_numbers
            foreign_ref_numbers) n0
          (enc n0 "known_string" (privk app1))))
      (recv
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number reg_role_number reg_ref_number)
          (cat n1 (enc n1 "known_string" (privk app)))
          announce_registrar)) (stor conf_mib announce_registrar)
      (send
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n2 (enc n2 "known_string" (privk cont)))
          registrar_noted)) (load conf_mib cell_spec_body)
      (send
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n3 (enc n3 "known_string" (privk cont))) cell_spec_body))
      (send
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n4 (enc n4 "known_string" (privk cont)))
          cell_spec_body))))
  (defrole foreign_registrars
    (vars (n0 text) (app1 name)
      (foreign_role_numbers foreign_ref_numbers unit_number
        conf_role_number conf_ref_number text) (cont name)
      (foreign_reg_mib locn) (n4 text) (cell_spec_body mesg))
    (trace
      (send
        (cat
          (mpdu_header unit_number foreign_role_numbers
            foreign_ref_numbers) n0
          (enc n0 "known_string" (privk app1))))
      (recv
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n4 (enc n4 "known_string" (privk cont))) cell_spec_body))
      (stor foreign_reg_mib cell_spec_body)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule scissorsRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (trans z2 i2)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)))))
  (defgenrule cakeRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)) (false))))
  (defgenrule no-interruption
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (trans z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule discreteAfter
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z2 i2) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)))))
  (defgenrule discreteBefore
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z1 i1 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z0 z1) (= i0 i1)) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_new_registrar-at-3
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "new_registrar" z (idx 4)) (trans z (idx 3)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_config_server-at-2
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "config_server" z (idx 3)) (trans z (idx 2)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_foreign_registrars-at-2
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "foreign_registrars" z (idx 3)) (trans z (idx 2)))))
  (defgenrule eff-config_server-5
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "config_server" z (idx 6)) (prec z (idx 5) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "config_server" z (idx 5)) (prec z (idx 4) z1 i))))))
  (lang (mpdu_header (tuple 3))))

(defskeleton mams_registrar_reg
  (vars (cell_spec_body mesg)
    (n1 unit_number reg_role_number reg_ref_number announce_registrar n2
      conf_role_number conf_ref_number registrar_noted n3 text)
    (app cont name) (pt pval) (reg_mib locn))
  (defstrand new_registrar 4 (cell_spec_body cell_spec_body) (n1 n1)
    (unit_number unit_number) (reg_role_number reg_role_number)
    (reg_ref_number reg_ref_number)
    (announce_registrar announce_registrar) (n2 n2)
    (conf_role_number conf_role_number)
    (conf_ref_number conf_ref_number) (registrar_noted registrar_noted)
    (n3 n3) (app app) (cont cont) (reg_mib reg_mib))
  (non-orig (privk app) (privk cont))
  (uniq-orig n1)
  (traces
    ((send
       (cat (mpdu_header unit_number reg_role_number reg_ref_number)
         (cat n1 (enc n1 "known_string" (privk app)))
         announce_registrar))
      (recv
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n2 (enc n2 "known_string" (privk cont)))
          registrar_noted))
      (recv
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n3 (enc n3 "known_string" (privk cont))) cell_spec_body))
      (stor reg_mib (cat pt cell_spec_body))))
  (label 0)
  (unrealized (0 1) (0 2))
  (maps
    ((0)
      ((n1 n1) (unit_number unit_number)
        (reg_role_number reg_role_number)
        (reg_ref_number reg_ref_number)
        (announce_registrar announce_registrar) (app app)
        (reg_mib reg_mib) (n2 n2) (conf_role_number conf_role_number)
        (conf_ref_number conf_ref_number)
        (registrar_noted registrar_noted) (cont cont) (n3 n3)
        (cell_spec_body cell_spec_body))))
  (origs (pt (0 3)) (n1 (0 0)))
  (comment "Not closed under rules"))

(defskeleton mams_registrar_reg
  (vars (cell_spec_body mesg)
    (n1 unit_number reg_role_number reg_ref_number announce_registrar n2

(defprotocol mams_registrar_reg basic
  (defrole new_registrar
    (vars
      (n1 unit_number reg_role_number reg_ref_number announce_registrar
        text) (app name) (reg_mib locn)
      (n2 conf_role_number conf_ref_number registrar_noted text)
      (cont name) (n3 text) (cell_spec_body mesg))
    (trace
      (send
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number reg_role_number reg_ref_number)
          (cat n1 (enc n1 "known_string" (privk app)))
          announce_registrar))
      (recv
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n2 (enc n2 "known_string" (privk cont)))
          registrar_noted))
      (recv
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n3 (enc n3 "known_string" (privk cont))) cell_spec_body))
      (stor reg_mib cell_spec_body)))
  (defrole config_server
    (vars (n0 text) (app1 name)
      (foreign_role_numbers foreign_ref_numbers n1 unit_number
        reg_role_number reg_ref_number announce_registrar text)
      (app name) (conf_mib locn)
      (n2 conf_role_number conf_ref_number registrar_noted text)
      (cont name) (n3 text) (cell_spec_body mesg) (n4 text))
    (trace
      (recv
        (cat
          (mpdu_header unit_number foreign_role_numbers
            foreign_ref_numbers) n0
          (enc n0 "known_string" (privk app1))))
      (recv
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number reg_role_number reg_ref_number)
          (cat n1 (enc n1 "known_string" (privk app)))
          announce_registrar)) (stor conf_mib announce_registrar)
      (send
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n2 (enc n2 "known_string" (privk cont)))
          registrar_noted)) (load conf_mib cell_spec_body)
      (send
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n3 (enc n3 "known_string" (privk cont))) cell_spec_body))
      (send
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n4 (enc n4 "known_string" (privk cont)))
          cell_spec_body))))
  (defrole foreign_registrars
    (vars (n0 text) (app1 name)
      (foreign_role_numbers foreign_ref_numbers unit_number
        conf_role_number conf_ref_number text) (cont name)
      (foreign_reg_mib locn) (n4 text) (cell_spec_body mesg))
    (trace
      (send
        (cat
          (mpdu_header unit_number foreign_role_numbers
            foreign_ref_numbers) n0
          (enc n0 "known_string" (privk app1))))
      (recv
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n4 (enc n4 "known_string" (privk cont))) cell_spec_body))
      (stor foreign_reg_mib cell_spec_body)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule scissorsRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (trans z2 i2)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)))))
  (defgenrule cakeRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)) (false))))
  (defgenrule no-interruption
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (trans z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule discreteAfter
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z2 i2) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)))))
  (defgenrule discreteBefore
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z1 i1 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z0 z1) (= i0 i1)) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_new_registrar-at-3
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "new_registrar" z (idx 4)) (trans z (idx 3)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_config_server-at-2
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "config_server" z (idx 3)) (trans z (idx 2)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_foreign_registrars-at-2
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "foreign_registrars" z (idx 3)) (trans z (idx 2)))))
  (defgenrule eff-config_server-5
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "config_server" z (idx 6)) (prec z (idx 5) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "config_server" z (idx 5)) (prec z (idx 4) z1 i))))))
  (lang (mpdu_header (tuple 3))))

(defskeleton mams_registrar_reg

    (foreign_role_numbers foreign_role_numbers)
    (foreign_ref_numbers foreign_ref_numbers) (n1 n1)
    (unit_number unit_number) (reg_role_number reg_role_number)
    (reg_ref_number reg_ref_number)

         (mpdu_header unit_number foreign_role_numbers
           foreign_ref_numbers) n0

      (send
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n3 (enc n3 "known_string" (privk cont))) cell_spec_body))

        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)

        (reg_ref_number reg_ref_number)
        (announce_registrar announce_registrar) (app app)
        (conf_mib conf_mib) (n2 n2) (conf_role_number conf_role_number)

(defprotocol mams_registrar_reg basic
  (defrole new_registrar
    (vars
      (n1 unit_number reg_role_number reg_ref_number announce_registrar
        text) (app name) (reg_mib locn)
      (n2 conf_role_number conf_ref_number registrar_noted text)
      (cont name) (n3 text) (cell_spec_body mesg))
    (trace
      (send
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number reg_role_number reg_ref_number)
          (cat n1 (enc n1 "known_string" (privk app)))
          announce_registrar))
      (recv
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n2 (enc n2 "known_string" (privk cont)))
          registrar_noted))
      (recv
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n3 (enc n3 "known_string" (privk cont))) cell_spec_body))
      (stor reg_mib cell_spec_body)))
  (defrole config_server
    (vars (n0 text) (app1 name)
      (foreign_role_numbers foreign_ref_numbers n1 unit_number
        reg_role_number reg_ref_number announce_registrar text)
      (app name) (conf_mib locn)
      (n2 conf_role_number conf_ref_number registrar_noted text)
      (cont name) (n3 text) (cell_spec_body mesg) (n4 text))
    (trace
      (recv
        (cat
          (mpdu_header unit_number foreign_role_numbers
            foreign_ref_numbers) n0
          (enc n0 "known_string" (privk app1))))
      (recv
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number reg_role_number reg_ref_number)
          (cat n1 (enc n1 "known_string" (privk app)))
          announce_registrar)) (stor conf_mib announce_registrar)
      (send
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n2 (enc n2 "known_string" (privk cont)))
          registrar_noted)) (load conf_mib cell_spec_body)
      (send
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n3 (enc n3 "known_string" (privk cont))) cell_spec_body))
      (send
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n4 (enc n4 "known_string" (privk cont)))
          cell_spec_body))))
  (defrole foreign_registrars
    (vars (n0 text) (app1 name)
      (foreign_role_numbers foreign_ref_numbers unit_number
        conf_role_number conf_ref_number text) (cont name)
      (foreign_reg_mib locn) (n4 text) (cell_spec_body mesg))
    (trace
      (send
        (cat
          (mpdu_header unit_number foreign_role_numbers
            foreign_ref_numbers) n0
          (enc n0 "known_string" (privk app1))))
      (recv
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n4 (enc n4 "known_string" (privk cont))) cell_spec_body))
      (stor foreign_reg_mib cell_spec_body)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule scissorsRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (trans z2 i2)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)))))
  (defgenrule cakeRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)) (false))))
  (defgenrule no-interruption
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (trans z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule discreteAfter
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z2 i2) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)))))
  (defgenrule discreteBefore
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z1 i1 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z0 z1) (= i0 i1)) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_new_registrar-at-3
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "new_registrar" z (idx 4)) (trans z (idx 3)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_config_server-at-2
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "config_server" z (idx 3)) (trans z (idx 2)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_foreign_registrars-at-2
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "foreign_registrars" z (idx 3)) (trans z (idx 2)))))
  (defgenrule eff-config_server-5
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "config_server" z (idx 6)) (prec z (idx 5) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "config_server" z (idx 5)) (prec z (idx 4) z1 i))))))
  (lang (mpdu_header (tuple 3))))

(defskeleton mams_registrar_reg
  (vars (cell_spec_body mesg)
    (n0 foreign_role_numbers foreign_ref_numbers unit_number
      conf_role_number conf_ref_number n4 text) (app1 cont name)
    (pt pval) (foreign_reg_mib locn))
  (defstrand foreign_registrars 3 (cell_spec_body cell_spec_body)
    (n0 n0) (foreign_role_numbers foreign_role_numbers)
    (foreign_ref_numbers foreign_ref_numbers) (unit_number unit_number)
    (conf_role_number conf_role_number)
    (conf_ref_number conf_ref_number) (n4 n4) (app1 app1) (cont cont)
    (foreign_reg_mib foreign_reg_mib))
  (non-orig (privk app1) (privk cont))
  (uniq-orig n0)
  (traces
    ((send
       (cat
         (mpdu_header unit_number foreign_role_numbers
           foreign_ref_numbers) n0
         (enc n0 "known_string" (privk app1))))
      (recv
        (cat (mpdu_header unit_number conf_role_number conf_ref_number)
          (cat n4 (enc n4 "known_string" (privk cont))) cell_spec_body))
      (stor foreign_reg_mib (cat pt cell_spec_body))))
  (label 98)
  (unrealized (0 1))
  (maps
    ((0)
      ((n0 n0) (foreign_role_numbers foreign_role_numbers)
        (foreign_ref_numbers foreign_ref_numbers) (app1 app1)
        (unit_number unit_number) (conf_role_number conf_role_number)
        (conf_ref_number conf_ref_number) (cont cont)
        (cell_spec_body cell_spec_body) (n4 n4)
        (foreign_reg_mib foreign_reg_mib))))
  (origs (pt (0 2)) (n0 (0 0)))
  (comment "Not closed under rules"))

(defskeleton mams_registrar_reg
  (vars (cell_spec_body mesg)
